# FE8U (Fire Emblem: The Sacred Stones, USA) vanilla ROM address/symbol table.
# Extracted from the sibling full-decomp project's fireemblem8.map (GNU ld map
# format), which builds byte-identical to the real FE8U ROM. Useful for mapping
# a raw ROM/EWRAM address (e.g. from an old FEBuilder-style .event ROM patch, or
# any other artifact that only has hex addresses) back to the already-decompiled
# C function/global it refers to in this project's own src/include tree -- most
# vanilla FE8U code and data keeps the same address across both projects, since
# this project's legacy/archival build path is itself agbcc-based and layout-
# compatible.
#
# Format: one '0xADDRESS SymbolName' pair per line, sorted by address. A ROM
# code/data address starts with 0x08 (thumb functions have their LSB set to 1,
# strip it -- addr & ~1 -- before using it as an actual load address); an EWRAM
# address starts with 0x02.
#
# To find the function/global containing an arbitrary address, find the entry
# with the largest address <= your target (a simple bisect over this file).
#
# To disassemble the vanilla ROM at a given address, see
# scripts/modernize/disasm_baserom.sh (requires baserom.gba and arm-none-eabi-
# objdump, neither of which this file itself depends on).
